Derived: every AUTHORED documentation page an instance publishes, with the title and description its own front matter declares. Sub-graph smart-trust/docs.

This is an index, not the documentation. It says what exists and what each artefact declares about itself. What a process is for, when you would be in it, and what it is not, is authored beside it and reuses these entries rather than restating them — see the auto-docs skill.
